SELECT [e].[ExamId], [e].[CourseId], [e].[CreatedAt], [e].[Description], [e].[DurationMinutes], [e].[IsActive], [e].[LastUpdated], [e].[PassingScorePercent], [e].[ShuffleOptions], [e].[ShuffleQuestions], [e].[Title], [c].[CourseId], [c].[CreateDate], [c].[Description], [c].[DiscountPrice], [c].[DurationHours], [c].[ImageName], [c].[IsActive], [c].[IsBundle], [c].[IsDiscountActive], [c].[IsListedForSale], [c].[LastUpdate], [c].[Price], [c].[Title], [e0].[QuestionId], [e0].[ExamId], [e0].[Explanation], [e0].[OrderIndex], [e0].[Points], [e0].[QuestionText], [s].[AttemptId], [s].[EndTime], [s].[ExamId], [s].[IsCompleted], [s].[IsPassed], [s].[Score], [s].[ScorePercent], [s].[StartTime], [s].[TotalPoints], [s].[UserId], [s].[UserId0]
FROM [Exams] AS [e]
LEFT JOIN [Courses] AS [c] ON [e].[CourseId] = [c].[CourseId]
LEFT JOIN [ExamQuestions] AS [e0] ON [e].[ExamId] = [e0].[ExamId]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [e1].[AttemptId], [e1].[EndTime], [e1].[ExamId], [e1].[IsCompleted], [e1].[IsPassed], [e1].[Score], [e1].[ScorePercent], [e1].[StartTime], [e1].[TotalPoints], [e1].[UserId], [u0].[UserId] AS [UserId0]
    FROM [ExamAttempts] AS [e1]
    INNER JOIN (
        SELECT [u].[UserId], [u].[IsDelete]
        FROM [Users] AS [u]
        WHERE [u].[IsDelete] = CAST(0 AS bit)
    ) AS [u0] ON [e1].[UserId] = [u0].[UserId]
    WHERE [u0].[IsDelete] = CAST(0 AS bit)
) AS [s] ON [e].[ExamId] = [s].[ExamId]
ORDER BY [e].[CreatedAt] DESC, [e].[ExamId], [c].[CourseId], [e0].[QuestionId], [s].[AttemptId]

    SELECT TOP(1) [c].[CourseId], [c].[CreateDate], [c].[Description], [c].[DiscountPrice], [c].[DurationHours], [c].[ImageName], [c].[IsActive], [c].[IsBundle], [c].[IsDiscountActive], [c].[IsListedForSale], [c].[LastUpdate], [c].[Price], [c].[Title]
    FROM [Courses] AS [c]
    WHERE [c].[CourseId] = @courseId AND [c].[IsActive] = CAST(1 AS bit)
) AS [c4]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [c0].[VideoId], [c0].[CourseId], [c0].[CreateDate], [c0].[Description], [c0].[DurationSeconds], [c0].[IsActive], [c0].[IsFree], [c0].[LastUpdate], [c0].[OrderIndex], [c0].[Title], [c0].[VideoFileName]
    FROM [CourseVideos] AS [c0]
    WHERE [c0].[IsActive] = CAST(1 AS bit)
) AS [c5] ON [c4].[CourseId] = [c5].[CourseId]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [c1].[PdfId], [c1].[CourseId], [c1].[CreateDate], [c1].[FileName], [c1].[IsActive], [c1].[LastUpdate], [c1].[OriginalFileName], [c1].[Price], [c1].[Title]
    FROM [CoursePdfs] AS [c1]
    WHERE [c1].[IsActive] = CAST(1 AS bit)
) AS [c6] ON [c4].[CourseId] = [c6].[CourseId]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [c2].[Id], [c2].[AddedDate], [c2].[BundleId], [c2].[CourseId], [c2].[OrderIndex], [c3].[CourseId] AS [CourseId0], [c3].[CreateDate], [c3].[Description], [c3].[DiscountPrice], [c3].[DurationHours], [c3].[ImageName], [c3].[IsActive], [c3].[IsBundle], [c3].[IsDiscountActive], [c3].[IsListedForSale], [c3].[LastUpdate], [c3].[Price], [c3].[Title]
    FROM [CourseBundleItems] AS [c2]
    INNER JOIN [Courses] AS [c3] ON [c2].[BundleId] = [c3].[CourseId]
) AS [s] ON [c4].[CourseId] = [s].[CourseId]
ORDER BY [c4].[CourseId], [c5].[VideoId], [c6].[PdfId], [s].[Id]
